実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移

予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公的固定資本形成は、前期比▲2.4％と予測する。 
公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として 輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。 
2026年 7-9月期の地域別輸出数量指数を季節調整
値（当研究所による試算値）でみると、米国向けが
前期比 0.9％（4-6月期：同 2.8％） 、EU向けが前期
比▲2.4％（4-6月期：同 0.3％） 、アジア向けが前期
比▲0.3％（4-6 月期：同▲0.8％） 、うち中国向けが

### Page 1

M
Update
Winbond Electronics Corp  | Asia Pacific
Strong 3Q margins; growth to
moderate in 4Q
3Q26 earnings beat: 3Q revenue was NT$82bn, +38% Q/Q, +278% Y/Y, 8% below
MSe; GM was 70.6%, +4.3ppt Q/Q, +23.9ppt Y/Y, 12.5ppt above MSe; EPS was NT
$7.49, +39% Q/Q, +1045% Y/Y, 3% above MSe. CMS revenue grew 50% Q/Q as ASP
increased by ~60%, despite lower bit shipments, which was due to reserving supply
for L TA customers. Flash revenue rose 34% Q/Q on pricing and mix with flat bit

shipments. Memory GM reached 73.9%.
4Q growth to moderate; 16nm supports 2027: Management expects continued 4Q
growth, but a slower pace as non-AI demand softens. DRAM contract prices should
rise more moderately than in 3Q, while high density NOR remains tight. KH module
A capacity should reach 24-25kwpm by end-2026. 16nm ramp from ~2kwpm to
16kwpm is expected to support 2H27 shipments.
AI opportunities broaden; module  B capex approved: Management projects server

NOR bit shipments to grow 67% Y/Y in 2027. CUBE customers are increasingly
inference accelerator developers with several expected to begin small volume
production in 2027. Silicon capacitor capacity is largely allocated, but current
margins remain below conventional DRAM. The board newly approved NT$73bn
capex for the previously announced Module B expansion.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Winbond Electronics Corp (2344.TW)
Base case, 2.9x 2027e P/B. This method is in line with the approach we adopt for Greater China
memory IDM peers, in view of the industry's high volatility. We believe the stock merits a mul-
tiple beyond the high end of its historical band of 0.5-1.5x since 2017, as we are seeing DRAM
pricing upside, a sustainable NOR price hike, and opportunities in SLC NAND and SiCap
foundry business.

Risks to Upside
n NOR Flash pricing increase, driven by stronger-than-expected demand
n Stronger-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing supply shortage
n Faster-than-expected SLC NAND development
Risks to Downside
n NOR Flash downcycle, driven by weaker demand
n Lower-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing oversupply
n Slower-than-expected SLC NAND development

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

GUC (3443.TW)

GUC
Company description
Founded in 1998, Global Unichip (GUC) specializes in providing IC design
services, particularly in ASIC service. As a subsidiary of TSMC, which holds
34.8% of GUC, it offers a wide range of design services including spec-in and
SoC integration, physical implementation, 2.5D/3D packaging technologies,
mass production services, and advanced technologies such as high
bandwidth memory (HBM) and chip to chip interconnect IP .  With its

diversified global presence – Taiwan, North America, China, Europe, South
Korea, and Japan – the company offers services including mass production,
NRE, customer wafer verification projects, and IP licensing. Mass production
services provide customers with complete service, from back-end design
completion to wafer manufacturing, testing and management.
Investment strategy
We rate GUC stock as Buy/High Risk. We are seeing strong growth from its
CPU projects with two leading CSP customers. We also see strong order

potential from its EV clients. Long-term, we still believe back-end design
capability and 3DIC system integration will be critical to AI accelerators,
which would give better opportunities for TSMC 3DFabric partners with GUC
also being a beneficiary, in our view.
Valuation
Our target price for GUC of NT$8,000 (rounded) is based on 37x PER to the
average of our 2027 /28E EPS, slightly above its historical 3-year PER average
which we believe is justified by its strong earnings outlook supported by solid

demand from its AI projects with two US leading CSPs and growth upside
from its US EV clients. Our target price equates to 2027 /28E PB of 30x/14x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the GUC stock High Risk for its share-price
volatility. Downside risks that could mean the stock trades below our target
price include: 1) slower projects demand from US CSP customers, 2) lower-
than-expected NRE demand from less AI ASIC projects, and 3) delayed or less

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

Summary: NYPCB released its 3Q26 results after market hours on 5 November. Net profit 
was TWD3.30bn (EPS: TWD5.10), 9% below our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus. 
Revenue rose 21% QoQ and 49% YoY to TWD16.38bn, in line with expectations. GM was 
29.3%, 1.0ppt below our estimate and 0.7ppt below consensus, reflecting yield losses 
during the Kunshan ramp-up and a more modest BT substrate margin uplift than we had 
assumed. OPM was 26.4%, versus our 27.5% estimate and 26.5% consensus. Despite 
operating profit being broadly in line, net profit was weighed down by a higher tax rate 
(26% vs. our 22% estimate), reflecting increased profitability at NYPCB’s China factories.

Looking ahead, we expect modest BT and c.10% ABF price hikes in 4Q26, which would 
result in mid-teens % QoQ growth in 4Q26E. This is consistent with its October revenue of 
TWD6.29bn (released 2 November, up 13% MoM and 70% YoY), equivalent to 34% of our 
4Q26 forecast (historic range being 33-39%). Per management, the Kunshan plant’s UTR 
has increased to 90-95% in October (vs. 80-85% in 3Q26 and 65-70% in 2Q26). As 
NYPCB is unwilling to accept LTAs with end clients (compared with its competitors), we 
believe its bargaining power should be the highest among TW substrate vendors. We have 
a Buy (1) rating on NYPCB, with a 12-month TP of TWD2,460, based on a target PER of 
49x applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take on NYPCB’s 3Q26 results, released on 5 November.

• 3Q26 net profit missed expectations. Net profit came in at TWD3.30bn (EPS: 
TWD5.10), 9% below both our estimate (TWD3.63bn) and the Bloomberg consensus 
(TWD3.62bn). 3Q26 revenue of TWD16.38bn (up 21% QoQ and 49% YoY) was in-line 
with expectations, while the gross margin reached 29.3%, 1ppt/0.7ppt below 
our/Bloomberg consensus estimates due to loss on the yield rate as the Kunshan plant 
ramped up; our gross margin expansion assumption for BT substrate was also too 
bullish. Operating margin reached 26.4%, versus our 27.5% estimate and the street’s 
26.5%, mainly on the lower-than-expected gross margin. However, while the operating 
profit was largely on track with expectations, net profit was dragged down by a higher-
than-expected tax rate (26% vs. our 22% estimate) due to rising profitability from 
factories in China.

### Page 1

M
Update
Broader recovery supports record earnings. 3Q revenue was NT$597.3bn (+1% Q/
Q, +82% Y/Y), with gross margin of 3.6% (+0.2ppt Q/Q, -0.1ppt Y/Y, 0.3ppt above
MSe), and net profit of NT$10.3bn (+6% Q/Q, +170% Y/Y, 42% above MSe). Data-
center revenue fell 5% Q/Q on shipment timing, while industrial, automotive and
Future Electronics revenue increased sequentially. The richer mix lifted gross margin
19bp Q/Q to 3.60%; operating margin rose 18bp to 2.38%, with operating expenses

increasing only 2% Q/Q. Net financing costs rose 22% Q/Q to NT$1.94bn.
AI drives a stronger 4Q. Management guides 4Q revenue of NT$730-770bn, with
the NT$750bn midpoint up 26% Q/Q, as data-center and communications growth
outweigh seasonal declines across consumer applications, industrial and automotive.
The guided gross-margin midpoint falls 36bp Q/Q to 3.24% on mix, but operating
profit rises 19% Q/Q to NT$16.96bn as expenses grow only 1%. 4Q EPS guidance is

NT$8.07–9.05, with the NT$8.56 midpoint up 7% Q/Q. Financing costs of NT$2.6–
2.8bn and the larger weighted-average share base temper the translation from
operating-profit growth into EPS.
Growth broadens, but cash conversion matters. Future Electronics grew over 40%
Y/Y in 3Q and has started shipping data-center products. Management remains
positive on 2027 AI demand, without providing a full-year earnings forecast.
Inventory days increased Q/Q from 47 to 66  and net debt from NT$27.6bn to NT

$46.4bn, although longer payables kept the cash-conversion cycle near 19 days
versus 18 in 2Q. We see further earnings potential from AI scale and Future’s
recovery, with funding requirements and inventory conversion the key execution
risks.

### Page 4

M
Update
4
Valuation methodology
We cut our price target by 15% to  NT$296: We update our residual income model to
reflect earnings estimate revisions. We increase our equity risk premium assumption from
11.8% to 12.5% to reflect segment volatilities, and reduce our intermediate growth rate
assumption from 10% to 8% to reflect a more conservative medium-term growth forecast.
We keep other key RI assumptions unchanged: 2.0% risk-free rate, 0.9 beta, cash dividend

payout ratio of 85%, and a long-term growth rate of 3.0%. Bull case PT is cut from NT
$406 to NT$344.5, implying 10.6x 2027 P/E. Bear case PT is cut from NT$230 to NT$160,
implying 4.9x 2027 P/E. The risk reward is now skewed more towards the bear case due to
potential downstream demand disruption.
Exhibit 3: WT: Residual Income Model
Source: Morgan Stanley Research estimates

Relative  valuation
Our price target implies 9.1x our 2027 EPS estimate, in line with its 8.9x average one-year
forward P/E since 2018, which we see as fair given improving demand and the company's
continued progress in penetrating the AI supply chain.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

### Page 1

Earnings Review 
한화에어로 
스페이스 
(012450)

3Q26 Review: 악재의 중첩, 
4분기까지 지켜보자 
최정환 CFA

jeonghwan.choi@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 1,640,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
739,000 
방위산업

◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 1,640,000원 유지 
◼ 컨센서스 및 추정치 대비 매출, 영업이익 하회 
◼ 4Q26 및 27년 실적 추정치 상향

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 1,640,000원 유지 
4Q26E-2Q27E 지상방산 및 항공우주 EBITDA에 Target 12MF EV/EBITDA 25.0x 
적용, 상장 자회사 가치 SOTP 통해 목표주가 산출. 3Q26 실적 아쉬우나 투자포
인트에는 변함 없어 멀티플은 변경 없이 적용. 27년 실적 추정치 상향에 따라 본
업 가치 상승, 26년 스페인 자주포 양산 및 미국 자주포 시제 사업자로 선정되며 
현지화 전략이 유효했음을 증명. 3Q26 실적에 대한 우려를 소화하며 글로벌 피어
들과의 멀티플 격차 축소를 전망함

컨센서스 및 추정치 대비 매출, 영업이익 하회 
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 6.1조원(-6.0% YoY), 영업이익 4,520억원(-46.2% YoY, 
OPM 7.4%)으로 컨센서스인 매출액 7.4조원 및 영업이익 1.1조원을 크게 하회
하는 실적 시현. 하회의 주요 요인으로는 3Q26 필리조선소 적자폭 확대, 지상
방산 부문 실적이 부진한 영향. 지상방산 부문의 경우 매출액 1.2조원(-41.0% 
YoY), 영업이익 220억원(-96.2% YoY, OPM 1.7%)을 시현했는데, 폴란드 관련 
매출이 부재했던 1Q26 영업이익률 16.9%보다도 낮은 실적을 보임. 이는 국내
외 사업 모두에서 악재가 겹쳐 발생했던 것으로 보이는데 국내 사업에서는 마
진율이 낮은 사업이 매출로 인식되었고 수출 사업에서는 폴란드 매출 부재 뿐 
아니라 이집트, 호주 K9 자주포 매출 인식 또한 예상 대비 부진해 고정비 레버
리지 효과로 3Q26 영업이익률이 크게 훼손된 것으로 추정. 이외 대전사업장 복
구에 따른 비용, 기타 사업 충당금 등 총 500억원 수준의 일회성 비용이 발생했
으며 영업외손익에서는 원화 강세에 따른 환손실이 주로 반영되었음

4Q26 및 27년 실적 추정치 상향 
1Q26에서 3Q26까지 이뤄지지 않았던 폴란드 EC2 K9 자주포 30문 인도는 
4Q26에 집중될 것으로 보이며 폴란드 EC1 천무 또한 기존 가이던스였던 26년 
천무 40대에서 60대로 인도 대수 상향이 이뤄짐. 이에 따라 4Q26 지상방산 영
업이익률은 22.8%로 회복을 전망. 27년에는 폴란드 K9 자주포 및 천무 인도 대
수 증가로 26년 수출 비중 58.0%에서 77.2%로 증가, 이에 따라 영업이익률 또
한 매출 믹스 개선에 따라 19.8%에서 28.0%로 개선을 전망

監管衝擊與選後美股展望
2026.11.06
美股策略研究

### Page 2

結論與展望
• AI監管風險：民主黨取得國會兩院控制權，AI監管疑慮升溫。2027年資料中心建設進度相對
穩定，但2028年約30GW規劃容量面臨潛在審批風險，恐影響部分專案投產時程。
• 產業影響預期：德州共和黨仍掌握州參議院多數，長期全面禁建面臨立法及司法制衡。過去
約20GW受地方建設限制的容量中，實際延期僅約1.5GW，顯示政策曝險不等於實際損失。
• 政策影響：民主黨可能透過國會制衡財政擴張，中長期若赤字及發債壓力下降，將有利美債
表現。美元預期短空長多，而美伊衝突降溫預期升高，可能使短期油價持續承壓。
• 醫療與零售：民主黨可能爭取恢復部分Medicaid、SNAP及ACA補貼，有助維持中低收入家
庭購買力，支撐消費與醫療需求；惟川普仍掌握關稅行政權，零售業成本改善空間相對有限。
• 美股後市展望：歷史經驗顯示，期中選舉後隨政治不確定性下降，美股通常呈現回升。過去9
次期中選舉後12個月，科技股有8次跑贏大盤，獲利品質、成長及低波動策略亦具相對優勢，
後續仍須關注AI監管落地及企業獲利表現。

### Page 3

AI監管提案增加，2028面臨較高審批風險
• 民主黨在期中選舉取得參眾兩院 ，並提出超級 AI禁令及資料中心暫停建設法案，分別涉及限
制AI模型開發，及暫停新建資料中心直至相關機制建立 ，引發對監管趨嚴與投資放緩的擔憂 。
• SemiAnalysis估計，美國2027預計交付的資料中心約有75%已動工，短期進度穩定；惟2028，
規劃多數尚未施工，若暫停建設政策擴大，約30GW新增容量可能面臨審批延後風險。

### Page 4

州級監管受立法制衡，政策曝險不等於實際損失
• 州長行政權仍受州議會及司法制衡 ，以德州為例，共和黨仍掌握州參議院過半席次，全面禁
建制度化仍需跨黨派支持；惟州政府可延續部分許可限制，短期專案進度仍可能受影響。
• 過去一年約20GW受地方建設限制的容量中，實際延誤僅約 1.5GW；預期監管趨嚴將提高開
發成本並延後部分2028專案，短期影響AI硬體訂單與交付，但長期算力需求未必減少。

### Page 5

國會制衡與美伊局勢緩和，牽動美債及美元走勢
• 民主黨掌控國會後，較可能透過立法及預算權制衡共和黨政策 ，降低進一步財政擴張空間。
短期政策不確定性可能加大債市波動 ，但中長期若赤字與發債壓力下降，將有利美債表現。
而AI投資貢獻美國經濟成長比例加大，故美元表現將與AI股趨勢相同，預期美元短空長多。
• 伊朗戰爭推升汽油價格成為共和黨選舉壓力來源 ，隨民主黨掌控國會提高延續軍事行動的政
治成本，加上美伊持續透過中東國家間接接觸，市場對衝突降溫預期升高，油價短期回落。

國會控制權轉向，醫療與消費政策迎來調整空間
• 民主黨可能透過債務上限協商，爭取延後或縮減 Medicaid、SNAP減支，並恢復ACA增額保
費補貼，有助維持中低收入家庭購買力與醫療保險覆蓋率，對民生消費、醫療服務偏正面。
• 關稅也是本次輪替重點，但川普仍握有貿易政策行政權與301條款等工具，整體有效關稅稅率
未必明顯下降；因此零售業獲利改善仍取決於企業定價能力及供應鏈調整成效。

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

spike feed acyclical inflation.

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.
Investment Implications
While some key variables are
complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic
purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and
ongoing capital investment suggests
that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.
Exhibit 3: Pulse Check on: Equity/Bond Volatility and Diesel/Gasoline Prices.
A) Bond and Equity Market Volatility Diverge. B) Pain at the Pump: Diesel Prices Surge Ahead of Gasoline.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

ICE BofA MOVE Index is a market-implied measure of U.S. bond market volatility.
Consumer Price Index measures the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a market basket of consumer goods and services.
Baltic Dry Index is a daily shipping and trade index published by the Baltic Exchange in London that measures the cost of moving raw materials like iron ore, coal, and grain by sea.
Volatility Index (VIX) is a real-time market indicator that measures the stock market's expected volatility over the next 30 days.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research– BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

Document 318: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ November 9-13

Source PDF: all report pdf/ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ November 9-13.pdf

Date: 2026-11-07

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: November 9-13
Date:    3 hours ago
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: November 9-13

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD:
November 9-13
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 7 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Monica Sauro / Unsplash

### Page 3

The S&P 500 fell 2.7% last week, and the Nasdaq dropped 4.9%, both
posting their fourth straight week of losses. Thursday’s 4.4% plunge in the
S&P 500, its worst day since April 2025, came as investors weighed what
the Democratic sweep of Congress means for the AI data center buildout.
Both indexes rebounded Friday on strong jobs data, with the S&P 500 up
0.3% and the Nasdaq up 1.4%.
October’s CPI (Tue) and PPI (Fri) headline this week’s data calendar. Ten

Fed officials are scheduled to speak, and the UK and Eurozone report Q3
GDP. Wednesday’s Veterans Day holiday closes the bond market. Here’s
more:
(1) Earnings season. So far, 88% of S&P 500 companies have reported
their Q3 results. So 60 companies still have to report, and 13 will do so
this week. The "Earnings Squiggles" for 2026, 2027, and 2028 are likely to
rise even further over the remainder of this year (chart). Currently, industry
analysts' consensus is that S&P 500 companies’ aggregate EPS will be

$366.60 this year, $426.30 next year, and $495.10 in 2028. In the past,
Earnings Squiggles tended to fall because analysts were too optimistic.
This time, they haven't been optimistic enough!
(2) Fedspeak. Ten Fed officials are scheduled to speak this week
(Goolsbee, Barkin, Waller, Logan, Schmid, Paulson, Hammack, Daly,
Williams, and Jefferson). These are their first remarks after the
Democratic sweep. These Fed heads are likely hawkish on balance,

especially after Friday's strong employment report.

### Page 4

In early October, the federal funds rate (FFR) futures implied 3.3 rate
hikes over the next 12 months and 1.7 over the next six. Since then, those
projections have fallen to 1.9 and 1.4 (chart). Friday’s employment report
lifted the odds of a December rate hike to 78%. We think the FOMC still
needs another 50bps of increases to reach a neutral FFR.
San Francisco Fed President Mary Daly told CNBC on Friday that “the job
market remains stable and solid, but not booming,” with real wages falling

as pay lags inflation. She expects the Fed “will have to tighten policy
further.”
(3) Inflation. October’s CPI and PPI reports will be released on Tuesday
and Friday, respectively. The Cleveland Fed’s Inflation Nowcasting model
projects that the headline CPI rose 0.24% m/m and the core CPI 0.21%
m/m. That would hold headline CPI inflation at 3.7% y/y and ease core to
2.5% from 2.6% (chart). October would mark the 68th consecutive month
of CPI inflation above 2.0% y/y. The model sees both accelerating in

November, to 3.9% and 2.8% y/y.
The Nowcast also suggests that October’s PCED inflation will remain
firmly above the Fed’s 2.0% target, with the headline and core PCED
inflation rates projected at 3.8% y/y and 3.2% y/y, respectively. Both are
expected to heat up a bit in November.

### Page 5

October's PPI inflation is likely to be hotter than September's headline and
core readings of 6.3% and 5.0% (chart).
One of the more troublesome components of the PPI recently has been
transportation & warehousing services, which jumped 16.0% y/y in
September (chart). Soaring diesel prices are fueling inflation.

### Page 6

(4) ADP & unemployment claims. Payrolls rose 165,000 in October, well
above the 65,000 consensus. The unemployment rate fell to 4.1% from
4.2%. ADP’s weekly employment reading (Tue) follows a four-week
average of 16,750 for the week ended October 10, down from 17,500
(chart). ADP’s monthly report showed private payrolls up 86,000 in
October, beating the 67,000 consensus.
Initial jobless claims (Thu) follow last week's 189,000, with the four-week
average falling to 194,750, the lowest since 1969 (chart). Continuing

claims were 1,718,000 for the week ended October 23, with a four-week
average of 1,709,000.

### Page 7

(5) Global. The UK’s Q3 GDP (Thu) rose at an estimated 0.7% q/q pace,
up from 0.5% in Q2, when real GDP was up 1.4% y/y (chart).
The Eurozone’s updated flash estimate of Q3 GDP (Fri) follows the initial
0.4% q/q reading. Real GDP rose 1.3% y/y in Q3, with Spain leading the
major economies at 2.7% and France down 0.1% (chart).

clause.

Document 434: 【國泰證期研究部】AI產業-AI造就新藍海，資安市場高速成長 -20261109

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】AI產業-AI造就新藍海，資安市場高速成長 -20261109.pdf

產業報告 
2026/11/09

1 
產業更新 – AI 產業 
AI 造就新藍海，資安市場高速成長 
What’s New –AI 孕育七大資安及監管威脅 
在 AI 應用持續發展下，許多威脅相伴而生，目前市場主要面臨七種資安及監管問題：1. 社交工
程攻擊產業化 2. 提示詞注入 3. AI Agent 權限濫用 4. Model Poisoning、模型竊取及 AI 供
應鏈攻擊 5. AI 輔助惡意程式 6. AI 基礎設施、雲端與跨國資料風險 7. AI 監管風險。攻擊除了
開始更加客製化外，更針對 AI 模型及基礎設施做發動，主要應對方案包含多因素驗證、使用 AI 
Gateway、提示詞注入測試、機器身分管理、特權存取控管。另外法規上也開始出現監管需求。 
Trends/Outlook –Securing AI 市場成長快速，針對威脅分點擊破 
預估全球 Securing AI 市場 2026 年到 2028 年從 28.35 億美元成長至約 77 億美元，兩年複合
年成長率約 64.8%，其中 AI 應用程式安全主要是保護 AI 應用本身，包含上線前測試、部署風
險管理、執行時防護等功能。AI 使用控管則主要針對管理員工使用第三方 AI 的解決方案為公
司的 AI 使用監督與資料外洩防護系統 。AI 治理平台為跨企業的集中式 AI 治理工具 ， 用來定義 、
核准及執行負責任的 AI 政策。AI GatewayAI 是應用與模型服務之間的中介平台，作為 AI 系
統對外連接模型時的統一管制關卡。 
Investment Summary 
AI 時代下面臨到的資安及管制威脅，相關解決方案的提供商將顯著受惠，Palo Alto Networks
（PANW）其以平台化方式整合網路、雲端、資安營運及身分安全。CrowdStrike（CRWD）使
既有 Falcon 平台成為企業 AI 環境的安全控制中心。Zscaler（ZS）：AI Agent 強化了存取限制
需求， Zscaler 的價值在於提供更細緻的存取限制、資料安全及安全流量控制。Okta（OKTA）
提供身分以及存取管理，未來身分安全的計價方式，可能逐步由人類使用者席次，擴展到機器
身分、Agent 數量和操作授權。台股部分，則較為分散，受惠企業除了純資安廠商外，包含資
安產品代理商以及協助企業的 AI 整合商，包含中華資安（7765 TT ）已發展 Agentic AI SOC
等產品 ， 試圖將既有資安能力擴展到AI 應用與治理 。 零壹 （3029 TT） 代理產品包含前述的Palo 
Alto Networks（PANW）若國際資安原廠持續推出 AI 安全產品，國內代理商預估將受惠於導
入及維護需求。精誠（6214 TT）可協助企業把分散的資料整理好、設定權限並做好資安治理。 
 個股 代碼

### Page 2

投資 顧問 
產業報告

2 
AI 造就新藍海，資安市場高速成長 
AI 孕育七大資安及監管威脅，市場急需解方 
在 AI 應用持續發展下，許多威脅相伴而生，目前市場主要面臨七種資安及監管問題：1. 社交工
程攻擊產業化 2. 提示詞注入 3. AI Agent 權限濫用 4. Model Poisoning、模型竊取及 AI 供
應鏈攻擊 5. AI 輔助惡意程式 6. AI 基礎設施、雲端與跨國資料風險 7. AI 監管風險。研究部以
下逐一探討風險來源及應對方案。

1. 社交工程攻擊產業化：傳統網路釣魚攻擊通常需要攻擊者撰寫詐騙郵件、建立假網站、研究
目標組織，再透過大量發送尋找受害者。生成式 AI 改變了這種模式，攻擊者現在能以較低
的邊際成本，產生不同語言、不同個人背景的詐騙內容，並利用公開資料建立高度客製化的
訊息，語音合成和 Deepfake 也可能讓攻擊從文字擴展到電話與視訊。

核心變化不是每一次攻擊都比過去更成功，而是攻擊者可以更低成本執行更多測試，並針對
高價值對象持續調整。根據數據 IBM 發布的調查指出，根據調查的樣本，裡面的惡意資料
外洩事件約四分之一涉及 AI 輔助攻擊，較先前增加 56%，平均造成約 600 萬美元損失，較
整體資料外洩樣本平均值高約 100 萬美元。

主要應對方案在於導入進階郵件安全、反釣魚偵測、抗釣魚多因素驗證、身分異常分析及高
風險交易的獨立覆核機制。但單純使用 AI 來辨認 AI 產生的語音或影片，不足以建立可靠的
金融交易安全機制，主因存在誤判和技術迭代風險。直接受惠的包括身分安全、郵件安全、
與資安教育訓練。

圖 1. AI 攻擊佔比提升

資料來源：IBM、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 3

投資 顧問 
產業報告

3 
2. Prompt Injection（提示詞注入）：假設企業建立一個能閱讀內部文件的 AI 助理，使用者可
以要求它整理財務報告，而當 AI 讀取外部網頁、電子郵件或第三方文件時，攻擊者可能在
資料內容中夾帶惡意指令 。 例如某份文件本身是低信任資料 ， 卻包含要求AI 忽略原先設定 、
搜尋機密資訊並傳送至外部網址的文字。當 AI 把這些內容誤認為具權威性的操作指令時，
就可能產生越權操作。因此，企業需要控制AI 可以呼叫的工具、存取的資料與執行的動作。
真正有效的控制點，必須在模型之外。

主要應對方案在於用最小權限、工具呼叫白名單，或使用 AI Gateway、輸入及輸出檢測、
提示詞注入測試，並保留完整的工具執行紀錄。

3. AI Agent 權限濫用：早期企業導入生成式 AI，大多以問答、搜尋及內容產生為主。但 AI 
Agent 可以進一步操作企業系統，例如讀取客戶資料、建立訂單、修改程式碼、執行雲端操
作，甚至安排金融交易。這使 AI 由提供資訊的系統轉為具有操作權限的系統。若其操作流
程受到惡意內容干擾，潛在損失可能遠高於聊天機器人產生錯誤文字。

其威脅類似第二項，但特色在於權限可能被長時間保留，且 AI Agent 操作具有連續性，責
任主體也不容易追蹤。機器身分管理、特權存取控管及即時授權變得更加重要。

4. Model Poisoning、模型竊取及 AI 供應鏈攻擊：AI 模型本身也可能成為攻擊目標。模型
供應鏈涵蓋訓練資料 、 預訓練權重 、 微調資料集 、 開源套件、推論框架 、 模型部署平台等等，
攻擊類型簡而言之包括像是在資料集加入惡意資料、透過存取或查詢取得模型能力、從模型
資訊推測敏感資料。對於自行訓練模型的大型企業而言，模型安全更接近軟體供應鏈安全與 
MLOps 安全(機器學習安全)的結合。但對於大部分只透過 API 使用現成模型的企業，最高
優先級通常仍是 API 權限、資料保護和應用程式安全。

5. AI 輔助惡意程式：AI 被濫用來加快惡意內容製作或攻擊準備。這使防守企業防護反應時間
必須縮短，當攻擊者能以更快速度掃描網路資產，企業若仍以每月一次的人工漏洞盤點方式
運作，可能無法及時處理高風險暴露點。其次，資安團隊容易出現告警疲勞，端點、網路、
雲端、身分和應用程式每天產生大量事件，分析人員可能無法有效完成，交叉比對。

因此，企業的需求會逐漸從傳統 SIEM（安全資訊與事件管理）的日誌收集，轉向具有行為
關聯、威脅情資及自動化回應能力的 XDR(延伸式偵測及回應)、Next-Gen SIEM、MDR(託
管式偵測及回應)。然而防禦端的 AI 也可能產生誤判，若允許系統無限制自動封鎖伺服器或
刪除帳號，甚至可能造成比原始攻擊更嚴重的營運中斷。合理的架構應依風險分級決定自動
化程度，將可逆且低風險的操作優先自動化，涉及生產環境與資金的高風險操作則需要核准
機制。

### Page 4

投資 顧問 
產業報告

4 
6. AI 基礎設施、雲端與跨國資料風險：AI 工作負載通常運行在大型雲端平台、GPU 叢集。相
關風險包括雲端權限設定錯誤、API 金鑰外洩、AI 推論服務遭 DDoS 攻擊及跨租戶資料隔
離失效。這些並非全部由 AI 才開始出現，但 AI 擴大了雲端工作負載和資料傳輸的數量，也
增加了使用者、模型與系統之間的互動。因此傳統防護仍有必要，而且需要支援 AI 工作負
載的可視性。

7. AI 監管風險：AI 監管不完全等於資安監管。兩者相關但法律要求必須分開看待。例如網路
攻擊、資料外洩及服務中斷屬於資訊安全問題，AI 歧視、禁止用途、訓練資料來源、可解釋
性與合成內容標示，則可能涉及 AI 治理和基本權利保護。某家公司即使資安能力很好，也
不代表它自動符合所有 AI 法律要求。企業未來需要保存模型與系統文件、建立風險評估流
程、追蹤重要變更、管理外部供應商，以及證明其控制措施的有效性。部分情境還需要人類
監督、資料治理、測試與事件報告能力。不過企業是否受到某條規定約束，須區分其角色是
模型供應商、AI 系統提供者、部署者、進口商或分銷商。

Securing AI 市場規模預測 
根據數據預估 ， 全球Securing AI 市場 2026 年到 2028 年從 28.35 億美元成長至約 77 億美元 ，
兩年複合年成長率約 64.8%，其中 AI 應用程式安全主要是保護 AI 應用本身，包含上線前測試、
部署風險管理、執行時防護等功能。AI 使用控管則主要針對管理員工使用第三方 AI 的解決方案
為公司的 AI 使用監督與資料外洩防護系統 。AI 治理平台為跨企業的集中式 AI 治理工具 ， 用來定
義 、 核准及執行負責任的AI 政策，相當於企業所有 AI 系統的治理及合規管理中心。AI GatewayAI 
是應用與模型服務之間的中介平台，提供集中式安全、治理及可觀測性，作為 AI 系統對外連接
模型時的統一管制關卡。其他 AI 安全則包括模型供應鏈安全、模型完整性保護、AI 專屬資料安
全與相關專業服務等等，相關預估數據如圖 2。

圖 2. Securing AI 市場規模預測

資料來源：Gartner、國泰證期研究部整理

5 
相關解決方案提供商將顯著受惠 
根據前述 AI 時代下面臨到的資安及管制威脅，相關解決方案的提供商將顯著受惠，以下分述各
廠商受惠原因： 
Palo Alto Networks（PANW）：其以平台化方式整合網路、雲端、資安營運及身分安全。在 AI
時代，企業需要保護的範圍增加，原本分開採購的產品之間也出現更多整合需求。例如當某個AI 
Agent 在雲端讀取敏感資料，再透過 API 傳送至外部服務時，資安團隊需要將身分、雲端、網路
與應用程式事件連結起來 ， 如果由不同供應商分別提供這些產品 ， 資料整合與管理成本可能提高 。
Palo Alto Networks 的平台化策略，正是利用這種複雜度擴大客戶的採用範圍。 
CrowdStrike（CRWD）：透過 Falcon 平台建立跨端點與工作負載的偵測能力，再逐步擴展至身
分、雲端、資安營運與 AI 防護，讓資安人員能處理更大量、跨系統且高度複雜的攻擊事件。公
司也已延伸至 AI Agent 身分及 AI Detection and Response，試圖使既有 Falcon 平台成為企業 
AI 環境的安全控制中心。 
Zscaler（ZS）：在傳統網路架構中，企業通常先透過 VPN 讓員工進入內部網路，再限制部分系
統存取，AI Agent 更強化了這種需求，因為未來存取企業資料的主體不再只有員工，也包括大量
非人類自動化流程。Zscaler 的價值在於提供更細緻的存取限制、資料安全及安全流量控制。 
Okta（OKTA）：Okta 的受惠原因來自 AI Agent 數量增加下，而每個 Agent 都需要身分及權限
管理。與傳統員工身分不同，AI Agent 可能短時間執行大量工作，也可能根據任務自動連接多個
資料來源，未來身分安全的計價方式，可能逐步由人類使用者席次，擴展到機器身分、Agent 數
量和操作授權。 
台股部分 ， 則較為分散 ， 受惠企業除了純資安廠商外 ， 包含資安產品代理商以及協助企業的AI 整
合商，包含: 
中華資安（7765 TT）：其業務包含 SOC、MDR、DDoS 防護、WAF、資安檢測、紅隊演練及安
全顧問服務。這些業務與 AI 時代的企業資安需求有高度重疊。更值得注意的是，公司已發展  
Agentic AI SOC，以及 AI Archway（AI 安檢通道）等產品，試圖將既有資安能力擴展到 AI 應
用與治理。 
零壹（3029 TT）：屬於資訊產品代理及企業 IT 解決方案提供商，代理產品包含前述的 Palo Alto 
Networks（PANW）若國際資安原廠持續推出 AI 安全產品，國內代理商預估將受惠於導入及維
護需求。 
精誠（6214 TT）：具有企業 IT 系統、軟體、雲端及資安整合能力，企業 AI 導入增加將帶動整體 
IT 專案，可協助企業把分散的資料整理好、設定權限並做好資安治理。

達發 6526 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
光通發力，再達新高 
投資與評價 
1. 達發 3Q26 受惠光通與乙太網持續放亮，且商務耳機出貨暢旺，營收創
單季新高，獲利優於預期；預期 4Q26 光通與乙太網延續成長，淡季不
淡，營運為歷史同期新高。 
2. 單通道 100G 開始出貨，預期 27 年逐步放量，10G PON 切入 AI 資料
中心 OOB，新一代 PHY 同步貢獻，上修 26 年及 27 年營收預估，預估
26 年 EPS 21.76 元；27 年 EPS 30.18 元，營運創歷史新高。 
3. 目前達發股價為 707 元，約 23 倍 FY27E EPS ，位於近四年本益比參考
區間 20-50 倍偏下位置。考量光通高速成長，網通切入全新市場，維持
買進投資建議；惟受上下游供給緊缺，毛利率上升空間有限，將評價由
平均值 35 倍調至中間偏下 30 倍，目標價 900 元(30x FY27E EPS)。

重點更新 
光通與商務耳機出貨暢旺，達發 3Q26 營運創近四年新高 
達發 3Q26 光通憑藉產能與交期優勢提升市占，單通道 50G 出貨優於預期，
單通道 100G 開始小量出貨，乙太網受惠低軌衛星規格升級與交換器市占提
升，藍牙受惠旺季及高階專案出貨，營收季增 10.4%至 69.41 億元，創單季
新高。毛利率 49.3%優於原先預估之 49.1%，費用方面控管得當，營業費用
22.87 億元(+0.6% QoQ)低於原先預估之 23.35 億元，營業利益 11.34 億元
(+20.3% QoQ)，稅後淨利季增 17.0%至 10.29 億元，EPS 6.15 元，營運創
近四年單季新高。

預期光通與乙太網延續成長，達發 4Q26 營運為歷史同期新高 
達發 4Q26 提前拉貨比重有限 ， 預期藍牙受惠商務耳機量產 ， 跌幅小於往年；
光通單通道 100G 逐漸放量，乙太網 2.5G PHY 與交換器延續成長，因此上
修營收預估 5.3%至 67.80 億元(-2.3% QoQ)，淡季不淡。 預估毛利率47.8%，
營業費用 21.60 億元(-5.6% QoQ)，營業利益 10.83 億元(-4.5% QoQ)，稅
後淨利 9.88 億元(-4.1% QoQ)，EPS 5.90 元，營運為歷史同期新高。

報告內容 
光通與商務耳機出貨暢旺，達發 3Q26 獲利優於預期 
達發 3Q26 光通需求強勁，在市場供給偏緊下，達發產能與交期相對穩定，單通道 50G 持續提
升市占，出貨優於預期，單通道 100G 亦開始小量出貨，50G TIA 並以獨立晶片形式出貨。乙太
網受惠低軌衛星客戶由 1G 升級至 ASP 較高的 2.5G PHY，輕管理型交換器需求增溫。寬頻以北
美成長最快，10G PON 比重逐步升高。藍牙則為消費旺季，電競及商務耳機等高階專案出貨增
加，3Q26 營收季增 10.4%至 69.41 億元(+23.3% YoY)，創單季新高。 
毛利率方面，DRAM 及 NOR Flash 今年漲幅達數倍，封測成本亦上升，產品漲價於 3Q26 貢獻
有限，惟產品組合優化部分抵減成本壓力，毛利率季減1.9 個百分點至 49.3%，略優於原先預估
之 49.1%。營業費用 22.87 億元(+0.6% QoQ)，低於原先預估之 23.35 億元，營業利益11.34 億
元(+20.3% QoQ)，稅後淨利季增 17.0%至 10.29 億元，EPS 6.15 元，營運創近四年單季新高。

光通成長淡季不淡，預期達發 4Q26 營運為歷史同期新高 
達發 4Q26 有別於傳統淡季，預期光通與乙太網延續強勁成長，其中光通單通道 100G 出貨增
加，為最大成長動能，乙太網則受惠2.5G PHY 持續導入 Wi-Fi 新客戶及新一代 10G PHY 量產。
寬頻隨北美設備商產品陸續落地 ， 並切入AI 資料中心 OOB 新市場 ， 藍牙則受惠商務耳機專案量
產，高階應用占比提升，預估淡季影響優於往年，加上下半年提前拉貨占藍牙音訊及寬頻營收皆
不到一成 ， 後續客戶庫存調整壓力有限 ， 上修4Q26 營收預估 5.3%至 67.80 億元(-2.3% QoQ)，
淡季不淡。 
毛利率方面，產品價格調整預期自 4Q26 開始反映，光通占比持續提升，惟調價依客戶及產品線
逐案協商，預期無法完全反映成本漲幅，且 NOR Flash 及封測成本持續走揚，下修毛利率預估
1.3 個百分點至 47.8%(-1.5ppts QoQ)。下調營業費用 2.1%至 21.60 億元(-5.6% QoQ)，上調營
業利益預估 13.2%至 10.83 億元(-4.5% QoQ, +127.1% YoY)； 稅後淨利9.88 億元(-4.1% QoQ，
+78.0% YoY)，EPS 5.90 元，營運為歷史同期新高。

光通營收大幅成長，預期達發 26 年營運創歷史新高 
達發 26 年光通受惠全球前十大模組廠滲透率提升 ， 加上AI 資料中心光模組進入三至五年替換週
期，預期營收成長至 25 年的四倍以上，占全年營收 mid-single digit ，產品以 12 奈米單通道
50G 為主，單通道100G 則於下半年開始小量出貨。寬頻繼25 年歐洲市場放量後，26 年由北美
接棒，1~3Q26 北美營收翻倍成長，10G PON 增速領先其他寬頻產品，乙太網則受惠低軌衛星
地面設備價量齊揚，非管理型交換器海外通路市占穩健擴張，預期為成長僅次於光通的產品線，
藍牙商務耳機 26 年營收預期翻倍成長，占藍牙音訊比重由個位數提升至接近兩成，帶動ASP 提
升，上修 26 年營收預估 1.4%至 254.66 億元(+21.7% YoY)。 
毛利率方面，1H26 受惠產品組合維持於 51.9%，惟下半年上下游成本皆大幅上漲，下修毛利率
預估 0.3 個百分點至 50.1%(-1.8ppts YoY)。下調營業費用預估 1.0%至 87.82 億元(+10.4% YoY)，
費用率因營收規模放大，由 25 年的 38.0%降至 34.5%，營業利益 39.76 億元(+36.8% YoY)；預
估稅後淨利年增 25.7%至 36.45 億元，EPS 21.76 元，營運創歷史新高。

### Page 4

100G 接棒放量，預期達發 27 年營運再創歷史新高 
達發 27 年光通動能由單通道 100G 接棒，驗證進度符合預期， 1H27 逐步放量，預估於 2Q27
占光通營收一半，100G DSP ASP 為 50G 的兩倍以上，100G TIA 搭載率預期高於 50G。預期 27
年光通營收成長至 26 年的三倍，占營收比重提升至 15%。中長期則有單通道 200G 目標於 27
年底 T ape Out，並投資元澄半導體以加速模組驗證與導入。寬頻方面，25G 及 50G PON 預期
於 27 年量產，OOB 目前以較低 PON 數配置為主，配置擴大後滲透率可望提升。乙太網新一代
10G PHY 搭配 10G PON、Wi-Fi 及 5G FWA 平台，預期 2.5G 交換器出貨增加。藍牙 6 奈米新
平台預期 26 年底 T ape Out，並往 B2B 應用布局，上修 27 年營收預估 3.6%至 306.74 億元
(+20.5% YoY)。 
毛利率方面，預期產品價格調整效益 27 年逐步顯現，光通占比亦持續提升，惟使用封裝及記憶
體較多之藍牙與寬頻等產品預估合計仍占營收七至八成，且達發身為後進者，光通定價以擴大市
占為優先，下修毛利率預估 0.9 個百分點至 50.1%，與 26 年持平。研發費用隨 100G、200G 
SerDes 及 25G PON 等新產品持續投入，惟 200G SerDes IP 授權金認列為無形資產並分期攤
銷，對費用影響有限，預估營業費用97.48 億元(+11.0% YoY)，營業費用率下降至 31.8%，營業
利益 56.10 億元(+41.1% YoY)；預估稅後淨利年增 38.7%至 50.56 億元，EPS 30.18 元，營運再
創歷史新高。

100G 放量在即，達發成長動能升溫 
達發過去以消費性藍牙與家用寬頻為主，目前有線網通基礎建設營收占比已達約五成，預期 27
年持續提升，AI 資料中心相關營收比重加速擴大。高速光通、OOB 及乙太網 PHY 同步切入 AI
基礎建設，客戶由模組廠延伸至大型雲端業者，產品週期波動逐步降低，預估26 年及 27 年 EPS
分別為 21.76 元及 30.18 元，成長動能明確。 
目前達發股價為 707 元(23X FY27E EPS)，位於近四年本益比參考區間 20-50 倍下緣位置，考量
光通成長動能預期延續，OOB 架構打開 AI 資料中心新應用，維持買進投資評等。惟 27 年毛利
率預估持平於 50.1%，毛利率仍受上下游供給緊缺影響，因此將評價基礎由平均值之 35 倍調整
至中間偏下之 30 倍，目標價由 995 元(35x 修正前 FY27E EPS)調整至 900 元(30x 修正後 FY27E 
EPS)。

### Page 5

公司簡介 
達發科技股份有限公司(Airoha Technology Corp., 6526 TT) 成立於 2001 年，總部位於新竹縣
竹北市，為聯發科集團旗下 IC 設計公司。2021 年整併誠致科技、雷凌科技、絡達科技及聯發科
IoT 部門並更名為達發科技，2022 年 6 月登錄興櫃，2023 年 10 月於證交所上市。公司業務分
為有線網通基礎建設與無線邊緣 AI 兩大事業群：有線網通涵蓋固網寬頻(G-PON、10G PON)、
乙太網(PHY 與交換器晶片)及光通訊(PAM4 DSP 與 TIA)，應用由家用寬頻、低軌衛星地面設備
延伸至 AI 資料中心光通訊與帶外管理，並於 2024 年 7 月完成參與九暘私募，九暘為合併子公
司，布局 PoE 晶片；無線邊緣 AI 以藍牙音訊為主，聚焦商務、電競及聲學等中高階耳機市場，
並延伸至藍牙傳輸(電競鍵鼠、電子貨架標籤)及衛星定位(GNSS)晶片。目前有線網通基礎建設與
無線邊緣 AI 兩大事業群營收占比各約 50%。

### Page 6

ESG 政策 
達發以社會、經濟、環境及資訊揭露四大構面推動永續經營，已在員工照護、職業安全、董
事會獨立性、資訊安全及溫室氣體管理等面向建立相關制度，評估期間未發生重大人權、消
費者權益、職業災害、誠信經營及環境法規違規情事。

社會面：達發依循國際人權宣言制定人權政策並揭露執行結果，並將人權要求納入供應商評
鑑與稽核。員工管理方面，提供優於法規的休假、保險及身心健康措施，並以員工認股權與
持股信託強化人才留任；職業安全衛生管理系統已取得外部認證，並揭露工傷統計及減災目
標。另建立績效評估及能力發展計畫，設定員工多元化目標、揭露女性主管占比，並足額進
用身心障礙員工。未來可完善人權盡職調查流程，並揭露員工滿意度、訓練成效、性別薪酬
比及客戶滿意度。

經濟面：達發每年召開 2 次以上法人說明會，董事長親自出席股東常會，並揭露議案表決結
果。獨立董事席次達半數以上，設置永續發展相關委員會並由董事長擔任最高負責人，且定
期執行董事會外部績效評估。另制定反貪腐、反競爭、內線交易防範及檢舉制度，風險管理
政策經董事會通過，並設置資安專責單位、定期測試資安機制。後續可強化接班規劃、設置
提名委員會、提高任一性別董事席次至三分之一以上，並將永續績效納入高階主管與董事薪
酬。

環境面：氣候管理由董事會及管理階層監督，已揭露範疇一、二及範疇三排放，並設定量化
中長期減碳、節能及再生能源目標。達發亦制定水資源及廢棄物管理政策，揭露用水量及有
害廢棄物產出量，並將環境表現納入供應商遴選及稽核。惟尚未取得 ISO 14001 及碳盤查認
證，未來可補充 1.5°C 以下及 3°C 以上氣候情境分析、再生能源使用量及用水、廢棄物量化
減量目標。

揭露面：達發依循 GRI 準則編製永續報告書，揭露重大性分析及 SDGs 對應作為，並提供英
文版報告書及英文網站。後續可導入 SASB 對應揭露及第三方確信，建立利害關係人回饋機
制，並採用雙重重大性原則強化 ESG 資訊完整性。

2025 2026
自動車
エアコン
（2020年 = 100）
（年、月）
2026 年 10 月 5 日

調査レポート

今月のグラフ（2026 年 10 月）

制度変更が押し上げた耐久財需要

調査部 研究員 小林 啓介

2026 年 4～6 月期の実質 GDP における個人消費は前期比＋0.0%と横ばいにとどまったものの、耐久財は同
＋4.0%と堅調に増加した。特に自動車とエアコンの需要が大きく高まったことが、押し上げに寄与したとみられる。 
新車販売台数（除く軽）は、自動車の燃費性能に応じて取得時に課税される「環境性能割」が 2025 年度末に
廃止されることを受けて今年度に需要の一部が先送りされた結果、4 月に前月比＋25.7%と急増した。同様にエ
アコンの出荷台数も、2027 年度から省エネ基準が厳しくなり、製品価格の上昇が予想されることから、それ を見
越した駆け込み需要が生じ、4 月には前月比＋12.5%と急増した。 
もっとも、こうした制度・規制変更に起因する一時的な需要増は徐々に落ち着きつつある。自動車とエアコンは
いずれも依然として高水準ではあるものの、4 月をピークに減少傾向に転じている。雇用・所得環境は良好であり、
イラン情勢悪化の影響で落ち込んでいた 消費者マインドも持ち直しへ向かっているなど、 個人消費を取り巻く環
境は悪くない。このため、7～9 月期の個人消費は底堅く推移するとみられるものの、耐久財消費の一時的な押し
上げ効果が剥落することで、小幅な伸びにとどまる可能性があることには注意を要しよう。

図表 新車販売台数（除く軽）とエアコン出荷台数

会「家庭用（ルーム）エアコン国内出荷実績」

### Page 1

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   1

法說重點摘要

廣越 
(4438 TT, NT$56.2, 增加持股)

焦點內容  
1. 3Q26 稅後淨利年減 15%至 4.4 億元，或每股盈餘 3.90 元。儘管產品
組合優化推升 毛利率至 15.4% ，年增 0.4 個百分點，中東地緣政治衝突
導致運費增加，營利 率年減 0.2 個百分點至 8.4% 。 
2. 預計 4Q26-2027 年營收 將延續成長態勢 ，而 未來營業費用回降取決於
地緣政治衝突緩解 。中東戰爭 導致船期從過往五週拉長至七週，運費也
較高 (行經紅海，運費近乎翻倍 )。 
3. 預計 2027 年資本支出提高至新台幣 4.5-5.0 億元 (對比今年約 2.5 億
元)，主要投入越南新廠擴建及約旦生產線增加 。 
結論 
管理層 表示 ，未來戰爭緩解及油價回落將為消費者信心與訂單回升關鍵；
目前公司將以提高自身營運效率與獲利能力為優先目 標。

3Q26 財報 
 3Q26 稅後淨利年減 15%至 4.4 億元， 或每股盈餘 3.90 元。儘管
產品組合優化 推升毛利率至 15.4% ，年增 0.4 個百分點 ，中東地緣
政治衝突導致運費增加，營利率年減 0.2 個百分點至 8.4% 。 
 1Q-3Q26 營收年增 4%，符合預期。得益於不動產處分利益及匯兌
損失收斂， 1Q-3Q26 稅後淨利 年增 105% 至 7.2 億元，或每股盈餘
6.41 元。 
 3Q26 產品別營收佔比： 羽絨夾克 43%、樹脂棉夾克 34%、薄外
套/大衣 4%、針織品項 6%、其他 (長、短褲等 ) 13%。 
 3Q26 客戶別營收佔比： Adidas ( 德) 3 2%、Patagonia ( 美) 1 1%、
VFC (The North Face ，美 ) 20%、Nike ( 美) 7%、Under Armour 
(美) 6%、Aritzia ( 加) 4%、Montbell ( 日) 4%、Lululemon ( 加) 
3%、Helly Hansen ( 挪) 1%、Alo Yoga ( 美) 1%。 
 3Q26 地區別營收佔比： 北美 43%、歐洲 24%、中國 20%、日本
6%、其他 7%。

營運展望更新 
 預計 4Q26 營收及 獲利將延續成長態勢 ，2027 年訂單也不會差。  
 10 月營收 微幅年減，主 要受船期影響。預計 11-12 月營收將顯著
增長。 
 新客戶 Alo Yoga 訂單利潤率優於公司整體平均。  
 過去一年鴨毛價格盤旋在高檔，主因中國豬肉價低，導致養鴨 數量
大幅減少；但近月鴨毛價格有走降趨勢，對購料成本有利。

政治衝突緩解。

退還給供應商。  
 公司持續推進 Icebreaking 計畫，優化 越南及約旦 生產流程與接單
配置，提升效率及資源運用。 越南及約旦 1Q-3Q26 人均產值分別
4%、2%。 
 目標未來五年 ROE 提升至 20%，並維持配息率 70%。

盤後生效(即12/1生效)。考量Q3個股市值變化與排名升降等因素，推估可能
納入與可能剔除之個股列示如下：

2492.TT 華新科 
 深耕被動元件，領航高階應用新商機

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
華新科為華新麗華集團旗下被動元件製造商，主要產品涵蓋 MLCC、晶片電阻、RF 射
頻元件及電感等，應用於消費性電子、車用電子、通訊及工業設備。公司以 MLCC 與
晶片電阻為核心，並積極將相關產品朝高精度、高功率與抗硫化的方向發展。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 315 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作 ，390 
【大盤指數/股價】39702 / 258.5

➢ AI 伺服器與高效能運算為 3Q26 獲利成長來源，EPS 4.73 元 
華新科 3Q26 營收 138.21 億元，QoQ+22.2%、YoY+46.8%，受惠 AI
伺服器與 HPC 需求帶動高階被動元件出貨 ， 加上產能接近滿載及漲價效
益逐步發酵；受惠於產品組合優化、產能利用率上升與漲價效益， 毛利
率提升至 27.84%，QoQ+7.6ppts、YoY+11.1ppts；稅後 EPS 4.73 元，
QoQ+46%、YoY+117.2%，獲利強勁。 
➢ MLCC 產品供需緊俏將延續，X5R 與 X6S 漲價趨勢明確 
三星電機預計 4Q26 調漲 MLCC 報價，其中 X5R 漲幅約 25%～30%、
X6S 漲幅約 10%~20%，隨日韓大廠將產能轉向高階 AI 產品 ， 一般型及
中高容值 MLCC 供給趨緊，投研部預估 4Q26 將對華新科產生訂單外溢
效益，出貨量與 ASP 皆有望季增 10%，占比由 3Q26 47%升至 50.7%。
此外 ，AI 伺服器功耗提升帶動 PSU 及電源相關晶片電阻需求 ，其產能利
用率已達 85%~90%，B/B Ratio 達 1.3~1.4，顯示供需持續緊俏，預估
4Q26 晶片電阻營收達 78.6 億元，QoQ+10%，占總營收 21.6%。 
➢ 電容與電阻類產品年增幅預估仍最強 
隨 GB300 NVL72 轉向 Vera Rubin NVL144，單機櫃 MLCC 及電阻用量
預估倍增 ， 帶動華新科AI 營收占比由 2025 年的 12%提升至 2026 年的
20%，B/B Ratio 亦升至 1.8，訂單能見度約半年。展望 2027 年，公司
2026 年資本支出約 40 億元，較過往增加 3 倍，主要擴充 AI 及高階被
動元件產能，搭配產品漲價效益，預估 MLCC、晶片電阻營收分別年增
45.1%、36.7%，整體產品線可望同步成長。 
➢ 預估 2027 年 EPS 26.07 元，首次給予買進評等，目標價 315 元 
投研部預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 501.6 億元/680.69 億元 ，YoY+37.6% 
/+35.7%；毛利率為 24.5% / 29.67%，YoY+7.2ppts /+5.2ppts；稅後
EPS 為 14.59 元/26.07 元，目前 PER 約在 17.7X /9.9X，參考過去五年
PER 區間大部分在 10X~30X， 目前股價以2027 年衡量位於區間下緣位
置，考量 2027 年 MLCC 與晶片電阻等兩大產品線成長動能將延續，且
產品漲價方向亦十分明確，故投研部給予首次買進評等，目標價 315 元
(2027F PE 12X)。

2492 華新科 加權指數

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

公司簡介 
華新科為台灣主要被動元件製造商之一，隸屬華新麗華集團，主要從事電子零組件的
研發、製造與銷售，產品涵蓋多層陶瓷電容(MLCC)、晶片電阻、RF 射頻元件、圓板
電容、電感及其他被動元件，廣泛應用於智慧型手機、電腦、通訊設備、車用電子、
工業控制及電源管理等領域，除了台灣以外，在中國、馬來西亞、日本與新加坡等地
皆設有生產據點 。在產品布局方面， MLCC 與晶片電阻為公司主要營收來源，其中
MLCC 應用範圍涵蓋消費性電子、工業設備及車用電子；晶片電阻則包含一般厚膜電
阻及高精度、抗硫化與高功率等特殊電阻產品。此外，公司亦積極布局高頻 RF 元件
及高附加價值被動元件 ， 以提升產品組合與技術競爭力 。 在2025 年的產品組合方面 ，
MLCC 佔 43.5%、晶片電組佔 21.5%、射頻元件佔 17%、電感與其他佔 13.9%、圓
板電容佔 4.1%。 
圖 1：華新科產品組合佔比

資料來源：華新科年報、國票投顧整理 
AI 伺服器與高效能運算為 3Q26 獲利成長動力來源，EPS 4.73 元 
華新科 3Q26 營收 138.21 億元，QoQ+22.2%、YoY+46.8%，主要受惠於 AI 伺服器
及高效能運算(HPC)需求持續增加，帶動 MLCC、晶片電阻與電流感測電阻等高階被
動元件出貨成長，加上被動元件市場供需逐漸轉緊，公司旗下高雄、中國東莞大朗與
馬來西亞廠區產能接近滿載，部分產品價格調漲效益開始反映；毛利率 27.84%， 
QoQ+7.6ppts、YoY+11.1ppts，季增幅明顯上升主因為產品組合優化、漲價效益進
一步推升平均銷售單價 (ASP)，同時上升的產能利用率也有助於降低單位固定製造成
本所致 ； 營業利益率19.06%，QoQ+8.8ppts、YoY+13.7ppts； 稅後淨利率18.71%，
QoQ+2.8ppts、YoY+4.4ppts； 稅後EPS 4.73 元，QoQ+46%、YoY+117.2%。2026
年累計前三季稅後 EPS 9.66 元，YoY+228.4%。 
MLCC 產品供需緊俏將延續，X5R 與 X6S 漲價趨勢明確 
參考海外的同業韓國三星電機(005930 KS)， 於日前已宣布將於4Q26 針對其 OEM 與
ODM 客戶進行 MLCC 報價調漲， 其中一般消費型X5R 產品平均漲幅約 25%~30%，
AI 伺服器使用的高規格 X6S 產品則預計調漲約 10%~20%，顯示漲價已從通路市場
擴大至直接供貨客戶 。 此舉對華新科而言 ，X5R 產品受到的影響主要是供給排擠效應 ，
由於三星電機及其他日韓大廠將部分消費型 X5R 產能轉向 AI 伺服器所需的 X6S、
X7R 等高階規格，使得 1μF～22μF 中高容值消費型 MLCC 供給減少，訂單有機會轉
向華新科等台系廠商，進而帶動接單及報價改善；1000V 以上高規格 X6S 產品則因

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
AI 伺服器、電源管理及高速運算設備需求增加，具備直接受惠於高階產品需求與價格
上漲的機會，有助於華新科提高高附加價值產品營收占比。此外，X7R 產品因具備較
佳的溫度穩定性，廣泛應用於工業控制、車用電子及電源管理等領域，隨著日韓大廠
產能轉向 AI 應用，相關供給也可能受到排擠，帶動台系供應商的議價能力提升；而
X8R 則具備更高的耐溫特性，適合部分高溫及高可靠度應用，若未來 AI 伺服器與工
業電子對高耐溫 MLCC 需求增加，也有利於華新科產品組合升級。然而，目前市場較
明確的漲價幅度主要集中在 X5R 與 X6S，X7R 與 X8R 的漲幅尚待後續觀察。整體而
言，三星電機調漲價格對華新科具正面影響，不僅在於市場報價跟進，更重要的是日
韓大廠產能配置轉向高階 AI 產品後，將使一般型及中高容值 MLCC 供給趨緊，華新
科有望同時受惠於訂單轉移、ASP 上升以及高階產品比重增加，推升 MLCC 在 4Q26
的營收佔比。投研部推測公司相關產品出貨量可望季增 10%、ASP 季增 10%，營收
比重由 3Q26 的 47%上升至 50.7%。 
此外， 晶片電阻為華新科第二大產品占比，近期同樣也因AI 伺服器功耗持續提升， 使
電源供應器(PSU)、 電源管理模組與電流感測電路所需的相關電阻元件也隨之增加 。 若
回顧 2026 年 6 月舉辦的股東會，公司當時的各類電阻產能利用率已達 85%~90%，
B/B Ratio 已提升至 1.3~1.4，投研部推估相關產品在 2H26 的供需緊俏程度仍將延
續，故 4Q26 其有望貢獻營收 QoQ+10%至 78.6 億元，佔總營收比重為 21.6%。 
表 1：華新科生產的 MLCC 種類

資料來源：國票投顧整理 
電容與電阻類產品年增幅預估仍最強 
由於 AI 伺服器由 GB300 NVL72 轉向 Vera Rubin NVL144 時，單一機櫃 MLCC 用量
由 43 萬~324 萬顆增加 1 倍至 86 萬~648 萬顆，電阻數量亦由 15~18 萬增加至
28~216 萬顆 ， 在單機被動元件產值以倍數式放大的同時 ， 將使華新科AI 佔比由 2025
年的 12%上升至 2026 年的 20%。 因目前高階電容與電阻產品供需持續趨緊 ， 帶動公
司整體 B/B Ratio 再進一步由 6 月份的 1.4 提升至 1.8，平均訂單能見度約半年，部
分料號甚至更長，顯示市場需求維持強勁。展望 2027 年，投研部認為，公司今年投
入約 40 億元資本支出，該金額相較於過往平均約 10 億元水準大幅增加約 3 倍，背
後主要用於擴充 AI、高速通訊、高功率電源及車用電子等高階應用所需產能，將購置
生產線的機器設備與廠務工程，加上原物料、人力及建廠成本同步上升，促使公司啟
動 產 品 價 格 調 整 ， 預 期 將 共 同 帶 動 五 大 產 品 線 營 收 成 長 。 其 中 ，MLCC 預估
YoY+45.1%、 晶 片 電 阻 YoY+36.7%、 射 頻 元 件 YoY+19.3%、 電 感 及 其 他 產 品
YoY+19.5%，圓板電容則是 YoY+24.9%，顯示在需求擴張、產能擴充與產品漲價效
益逐步發酵下，電容與電阻產品挹注公司營收增幅最大，總體而言各產品線 2027 年
營運皆可望同步走揚。

5347.TT 世界 
 VSMC 進度優於預期

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 世界先進於專注於 8 吋晶圓代工，為成熟特殊製程晶圓代工廠，目前擁有五座八吋晶 圓廠，分別位於台灣與新加坡，12 吋晶圓廠將採合資模式並於新加坡建置。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 150 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，195 
【大盤指數/股價】39702 / 120

➢ 3Q26 符合財測，獲利高於預期 
世界先進 3Q26 營收 157.02 億元(QoQ+10.2%：YoY+27.2%)，出貨量
751K，季增 5%。產能利用率約 90%，毛利率 33.7% (QoQ+1.3ppts，
產能利用率＆產品組合改善)， 營利率22.1%(QoQ+1.7ppts)， 業外收入
2.91 億元主要為利息收入，淨利 30.99 億元 EPS 1.65 元(QoQ+4.2%：
YoY+82%)高於我們預估的 1.45 元。 
➢ 4Q26 營運維持高檔 
世界先進目前訂單能見度長達 4 個月，拉貨動能主要來自於 PMIC 及
mix-singal，目前 PMIC 營收占比已達 82%，預估 晶圓出貨量季 增
0%~2%，ASP 季增 3%~5% (價格調整)，GM 34%~36%，投顧預估世
界先進 4Q26 營收為 168.2 億元(QoQ+7.1%)， 毛利率35.5%季增約 1.9
個百分點，淨利 35.29 億元(QoQ+13.9%)，EPS 1.88 元，整體產能利
用率上升到到 90%以上(細線寬產能全數滿載)。 
➢ VSMC 有機會提前轉虧為盈 
VSMC 產能 44K(interposer、mixed-signal、 電源管理產品 、analog IC。
終端應用包括：高速運算 HPC、汽車電子、工業應用、消費性電子、行
動裝置。相關技術授權與技術轉移來自 TSMC)。將於 1Q27 開始進入量
產並逐季擴充產能，季度產能利用率維持在 90%以上，2027 年至 2028
年為 ramp-up 階段，於 2029 年 H1 建置完成 ， 由於interposer 產品佔
比超越一半，目前 VSMC 損平點將低於 30K 月產能。 
➢ 展望樂觀，調升至買進評等 
世界先進目前訂單能見度拉長到 4 個月，而VSMC 在客戶技轉、投資規
模縮小狀況下將有機會提前轉虧為盈，2026 年 AI 營收占比已經超越兩
位數，2027 年將進一步增加，且價格也可反應成本的增加，投顧預估世
界先進 2026 年/2027 年營收分別為 592.98 億元/935 億元，EPS 分別
為 6.31 元/6.79 元優異水準，過去 PE 區間為 7~30 倍，調升至買進評
等，目標價 150 元(2027F PE 22X EPS)。

3Q26 符合財測，獲利高於預期 
世界先進 3Q26 營收 157.02 億元(QoQ+10.2%：YoY+27.2%)，出貨量 751K，季增
5%。產能利用率約 90%，毛利率 33.7% (QoQ+1.3ppts，產能利用率＆產品組合改
善)， 營利率22.1% (QoQ+1.7ppts)， 業外收入2.91 億元主要為利息收入 ，淨利 30.99
億元 EPS 1.65 元(QoQ+4.2%：YoY+82%)高於我們預估的 1.45 元。製程別：0.18µm 
and Advanced 佔營收 62%(季增 2 個百分點)；0.25µm、0.35µm、0.5µm 分別佔 14%、
12%、12%。平臺別：電源管理佔 82%(季增 5 個百分點)，大尺寸面板驅動 IC 10%、
小尺寸面板驅動 IC 3%、其他 5%。 
4Q26 營運維持高檔 
世界先進目前訂單能見度長達 4 個月，拉貨動能主要來自於 PMIC 及 mix-singal，目
前 PMIC 營收占比已達 82%，預估晶圓出貨量季增 0~2%(受瓶頸設備產能限制，整體
產 出 僅 能 小 幅 提 升) ，ASP 季 增 3%~5%( 反 映 成 本 轉 嫁 與 有 利 產 品 組 合) ，GM 
34%~36%，投顧預估世界先進 4Q26 營收為 168.2 億元(QoQ+7.1%)， 毛利率35.5%
季增約 1.9 個百分點，淨利 35.29 億元(QoQ+13.9%)，EPS 1.88 元，整體產能利用率
上升到到 90%以上(細線寬產能全數滿載)。 
VSMC 有機會提前轉虧為盈 
VSMC 產能 44K(interposer、mixed-signal、電源管理產品、analog IC。終端應用包
括：高速運算 HPC、汽車電子、工業應用、消費性電子、行動裝置。相關技術授權與
技術轉移來自 TSMC)。將於 1Q27 開始進入量產並逐季擴充產能，季度產能利用率維
持在 90%以上，2027 年至 2028 年為 ramp-up 階段，於 2029 年 H1 建置完成，由
於 interposer 產品佔比超越一半，目前 VSMC 損平點將低於 30K 月產能。AI 需求：
管理層認為 AI 算力需求趨勢確定且不可逆，美國期中選舉後的市場調整有助 AI 轉向
合理長期成長，而非過度集中資源的狂熱形態。 
成熟製程競爭：中國大陸同業擴張成熟製程，但因地緣政治與應用需求差異，直接競
爭威脅不大；部分國際大廠陸續關閉 6 吋、8 吋廠，8 吋成熟產能成長性趨緩，公司持
續關注並尋求機會。 
市佔率：PMIC 與 Discrete 在 foundry 中約居前三大至前五大；Discrete 全球市佔
約 10%~15%，在 foundry 端約 40%~50%。 
資本支出維持 600~700 億元 
2026 年全年資本支出預計落在新臺幣 600 億至 700 億元之間(其中近 90%用於 VSMC
新加坡廠建置與設備，其餘約 10%用於 8 吋廠例行維修及優化)；2026 年全年折舊費
用約為新臺幣 91 億元(年增 6%)。8 吋廠產能調整：2026 年持續汰換並升級部分現有
產能至細線寬製程，2026 年全年 8 吋晶圓產能預估約為 330.6 萬片(年減約 4%)；第
四季產能預估季增 1,000 片至 28 萬片 8 吋晶圓。終端市場狀況：車用市場持平穩定；
工業用工控市場穩定成長；消費性電子與 PC/手機需求相對疲弱，但對整體 90%以上
的產能利用率無顯著負面影響。 
展望樂觀，調升至買進評等 
世界先進目前訂單能見度拉長到 4 個月，而 VSMC 在客戶技轉、投資規模縮小狀況
下將有機會提前轉虧為盈，2026 年 AI 營收占比已經超越兩位數，2027 年將進一步
增加，且價格也可反應成本的增加，投顧預估世界先進 2026 年/2027 年營收分別為
592.98 億元/935 億元，EPS 分別為 6.31 元/6.79 元優異水準，過去 PE 區間為 7~30
倍，調升至買進評等，目標價為 150 元(2027F PE 22X EPS)。

Document 605: 宏观与大类资产周报：民主党“横扫”两院，外部扰动加剧

Source PDF: all report pdf/宏观与大类资产周报：民主党“横扫”两院，外部扰动加剧.pdf
Report source: 诚通证券
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 19
Status: ok

### Page 1

宏观经济
民主党“横扫”两院，外部扰动加剧
——宏观与大类资产周报（2026.11.02—2026.11.07） 宏观周报
 大类资产表现
A 股大势研判：上周，上证综指、沪深 300、创业板指分别上涨 1.4%、
1.7%、2.1%。上周市场震荡回升，主要宽基指数普遍上涨。从资金面来看，
截至 11 月 5 日，两融余额为 2.31 万亿元，较前周继续下降，但融资买入占
比小幅上升。交易量方面，两市日均成交金额为 1.86 万亿元，较前周小幅放
量。展望后市，外部风险扰动加剧，市场延续宽幅震荡。一方面，国内微观
流动性预期持续改善，央行释放了清晰的稳市信号。另一方面，民主党在美
国中期选举占据较大优势，掣肘特朗普团队政策出台与实施，短期内或进一
步加剧外部环境不确定性。总的看，美国中期选举后外部风险因素不确定性
显著增强，或影响美伊冲突局势、美联储货币政策路径、AI 全球产业布局，
对市场预期扰动较大，市场有望延续宽幅震荡态势。
A 股配置策略：把握超跌后修复性机会，但注重攻守兼备，围绕红利和

弹性两个方向布局。一是宽幅震荡中重视高股息红利方向，建议关注银行。
二是 AI 内容生成在应用端商业模式逐步清晰的传媒。三是具备充足修复空
间的国产算力与储能。
 宏观经济及政策动态
美国新增非农就业超预期。经济周期、经济动量均处于扩张阶段，不仅
AI 产业链，多数行业在扩张。受美联储加息及资产价格波动影响，金融条
件小幅回落，但限制性仍不强。10 月，ISM 制造业 PMI 从 54.5%上升至 55.5%。
新增非农就业 16.5 万人，失业率从 4.2%下降至 4.1%。截至 11 月 4 日，
GDPNow 预测四季度美国 GDP 环比折年率为 3.4%，据此推算全年 GDP 同
比增速约为 2.4%。
美国 PCE 通胀回升，通胀风险仍较大。9 月，CPI 同比增速从 3.4%上升
至 3.7%，核心 CPI 同比增速从 2.4%上升至 2.6%。PCE 物价同比增速从 3.4%
上升至 3.6%，核心 PCE 物价同比增速从 3%上升至 3.1%。不仅能源价格，
多类物价增速上升。年内美联储仍有加息可能。我们预计未来一年美国 AI

资本开支仍较稳健，经济周期或保持扩张，通胀具有较强黏性，美联储仍有
加息可能。截至 11 月 6 日，CME Fed Watch 工具显示，市场预期美联储 2026
年 12 月加息概率为 76.3%，2027 年加息一次。
 行业中观监测
上周，钢铁产业链开工率下降，化工产业链开工率上升；二手房销售上
升，新房销售下降；沥青开工率上升，水泥发运率下降；集装箱吞吐量下降。
10 月 1-31 日，全国乘用车市场零售 169.3 万辆，同比去年 10 月同期下降 25%，
较上月同期增长 5%。猪肉平均批发价降至 15.10 元/公斤，同比增速降至
-16.56%；蔬菜平均批发价降至 4.04 元/公斤，同比增速降至-29.85%；铁矿
石期货上涨 1.9%，螺纹钢期货持平前周，焦煤期货下跌 0.5%；黄金价格上
涨 5.0%，白银价格上涨 5.7%。
 资金面跟踪
上周，公开市场操作上周净回笼 16230 亿元，A 股主要股东资金合计减
持 1.1 亿元，前值为增持 33.3 亿元；上周全 A 换手率 1.17%（前值 0.98%）。
